Until World War II, the company as a legal person managed the estate in an appropriate manner. After the war under the decree of PKWN the estate was nationalized, the palace-park complex in Orłów became the property of the State Treasury. In the main palace there was a school, in the offices - health center and post office. Therefore, the team remained public in line with the tester's record. In the 1990s, the estate under the communalization carried out by Zamość Voivodeship became the property of the commune in Izbica. In 2006 it was unfortunately sold by the municipality of the Italian municipality to Giordani, which Daniel Richard Polanczyk, a man with an English passport, Polish roots, lived permanently in Italy, was to bring an offer from the Somali government to Lublin. He had, but to this day he merely provided the buildings with empty buildings and their progressing degradation. For a long time now, he has announced that these goods are for sale, but the price determined is the double value of what he bought them for. What business did the municipality make on this transaction? I don't think so. Thus, the story of the life of the Count, the great philanthropist, and his wealth is like an old drawer of beauty and a sad fairy tale that really happened, typing a trace into our national heritage. And national heritage is the work of previous generations, a resource of material and intangible things, a resource of spiritual and moral values. The object I am talking about is not only a national heritage in a material sense, it is a national heritage in the sense of human behaviour and attitude, which was an exception in terms of the preservation of the Polish land, because all the collected goods were transferred for social purposes. It is therefore a value that should be specifically cared for and protected from the passing of time. Today we are fighting for this value as a society in a special way connected with this historic, wonderful place. Our appeal is one of the forms of struggle, a search for a good host, and it is also an appeal to the Polish Government to develop a national programme for the protection of monuments protected by relevant legislation and a programme of responsible funding. As a Polish country, we must make this effort for the social good, it is a duty to past and future generations, otherwise we will lose many such palaces and other cultural goods, becoming the same people irresponsible to history. From this point on, I would like to thank all those who, in a selfless way, inspire us to work to save this monument. ParlaMint-PL_2016-06-21-sejm-21-1.u424	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! My statement as a resident of the Oświęcim district concerns the National Remembrance Day of the Victims of German Nazi Concentration Camps and Holocaust Camps. This day was adopted on 8 June 2006 by the Sejm of the Republic of Poland on the 66th anniversary of the arrival of the first mass transport of prisoners to the Auschwitz concentration camp. On June 14, he was chosen to commemorate the victims imprisoned and killed in concentration camps and extermination camps during World War II. On that day 76 years ago, the first transport of prisoners arrived at the newly established German Auschwitz concentration camp from the prison in Tarnów. He counted 728 men, most of whom were Polish political prisoners. Among them were soldiers of the September campaign, members of underground independence organizations, middle school students and students, as well as a small group of Polish Jews. They came mainly from southern Poland. Of these 728 prisoners deported from Tarnów to Auschwitz, 298 survived the war. It is worth recalling at this point that, according to estimates, in 1940-1945, the Nazis imprisoned about 150 thousand in Auschwitz. Poles. They were the second largest group of prisoners of this camp right after the Jews. At least half of these people died in Auschwitz or immediately after being transferred to other concentration camps. I would stress that in total more than one million people, including men, women and children, died in the Auschwitz concentration camp. 14 June 1940 is therefore a symbol of the beginning of the extermination of certain nations, especially Jews and Poles, planned by the German Nazis. Tarnowski's transport was the first to have a distinct mass character. It is worth noting that the National Remembrance Day of the Victims of German Nazi Concentration Camps and Holocaust Camps reminds and symbolizes above all millions of victims who lost their lives in German camps, and our fellow countrymen were among them. This year we celebrate the 10th anniversary of establishing a national commemoration day for the victims of German Nazi concentration camps. For this reason, let me remind you how important it is for the state and local authorities to take initiatives to commemorate the victims of World War II, in particular the Nazi German concentration camps and extermination camps. It is worth to emphasize the Polish contribution to the fight against the German ideology of national socialism and to the dedication of historical truth of the real nature of the camps and the crimes committed in them. This is also to remind young generations of historical facts, which are crucial to our nation and yet so often forgotten. Thank you. [[Applause]]

Young people who want to start their own families, as well as those who already have their offspring, will no longer be able to spread the spectrum of long-term credit. The order of award of these premises will depend on the fulfilment of certain criteria. However, we must remember that, as the Prime Minister said during her speech, this will only affect the waiting time. The Living+ programme is open to all Poles and responds to the need for anyone who is not able to rent or acquire their own place on their own for the time being, without State aid. Gradually put into effect the "Housing+" programme, as well as the successful implementation of the "Family 500+" programme, are expressions of the need for adequate effort, goodwill and listening to their needs to make changes that citizens expect. As a result, comprehensive solutions can be implemented in every area of life. The actions taken by the Law and Justice are therefore nothing more than the result of commitment and determined efforts, which aim to support all Polish families, increase the standard of living of our country's inhabitants and thus improve the demographic situation. It was for these reasons that Poles trusted the parties Law and Justice, and their trust was not and will not be disappointed. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2016-06-21-sejm-21-1.u432	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! On the last Sunday, 19 June 2016, in Janowiec, a village located in the Puławy district of Lublin Voivodeship, and Zwolenie, a county town in the Mazowieckie Voivodeship, there were celebrations commemorating the tragic events of 19 June 1944, when German invaders shot 41 people - members of the Home Army from Janowiec and Zwolen. On that day, the town was surrounded by police cordon, the residents were spent, the convicts brought in, previously captured in actions organized by the occupier and imprisoned in prison, brought to the wall of death and executed - surrounded by families, loved ones and acquaintances. The memory of the murdered was celebrated in Janowiec at Holy Mass in the parish church of St. Margaret and St. Stanisława. After the mass there was a laying of flower ties and inflammation of the bloom under a plaque placed on the walls of the church, commemorating among others. murdered members of the AK Janowiec facility. In the Call after the Holy Mass in the Church of the Exaltation of the Holy Cross, the marching column composed of participants of celebrations, banner postcards, representatives of the World Union of National Army Soldiers, the Polish Member, local authorities Zwolenia and Janowiec and the Zvolen district, ZHR scouts and students, residents of Zvolenia and surrounding areas and Janowiec, including family members of the murdered AK soldiers, passed to the National Memorial. The element of the monument is the wall of death, under which the murdered academy were killed. They were worshipped there, flower bundles were laid, and candles were lit in honor of the murdered. A further part of the ceremony took place at the parish cemetery at the murdered quarters, where a plaque commemorating six commanders of the Polish Home Army, and then the most active members of the World Union of National Army Soldiers, were presented with commemorative decorations for the merits of the World Army of Home Army Soldiers, headed by the chief initiator of the ceremony, Ms. Irena Kwapisiewicz. The inscription on the monument in honor of the murdered says: "He who has loved his people and freedom will not hesitate to sacrifice his life. Yeah, the memory of the murdered won't die. I personally worship them from this place as a resident of Janowiec, for whom the loss of my 20 citizens - soldiers of the Home Army has been a great trauma over the years. In addition, as unfortunately happened throughout the country after 1945, they were erased from memory, from the history cards, in this place creating the heroes of communist proverenity. Let my voice from the speaker of the Sejm of the Republic of Poland be at least a symbolic compensation for the harm suffered by their families. Part of the memory of the murdered on 19 June 1944 in the Call of the National Army soldiers. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2016-06-21-sejm-21-1.u434	Declaration on an increase in the amount reducing personal income tax, i.e. the so-called. the amount of the free, at the same time rationalising the tasks of local government units in terms of better management of revenue, grants and expenditure. High Izbo! In 2003, there was an increase in the level of decentralisation of public finances, the scope of the local authorities' competences increased significantly. When limiting corporate tax income (CIT) and individuals tax (PIT) lose the most local governments poorer (areas, municipalities and cities on the rights of the county) where poor or non-profitable citizens live. I hope that the tax-free amount will be increased. And it has to be done for Poles. I have an appeal to the government to take over some of the tasks, even in such sensitive health care. The expenditure of local government units (JSTs) is increasing, the capacity of local authorities to use grants is decreasing, there is a reduction in investment and there is a risk of fulfilling their own tasks. Without parliamentary consensus, only large and economically strong regions will survive, e.g. the localities where the successful bets are located, and the residents are employees. The provisions of the Act on Income Tax on Individuals setting a tax-free amount of PLN 3089 are incompatible with the Constitution of the Republic of Poland and are repealed on 30 November 2016 - as the Constitutional Tribunal stated. The provisions do not provide for a mechanism to correct the tax reduction amount, so that the minimum existence is not guaranteed. The proposal of the Rural Union of the Republic of Poland of 23 December 2015, which concerns the amendment of the income law of JST, should be cited as follows: "The share of JST in PIT tax should be increased accordingly or part of the revenue of the state budget from indirect taxes, such as tax on goods and services (VAT) or excise duty, should be transferred to the extent that it compensates for the planned loss of revenue. A similar position is adopted by the Polish Towns Association on 11 December 2015, but also adds: "Of course, other forms of compensation are also acceptable: reducing some of the financial burden of the JST or the scope of their tasks, but they have not been proposed. The Silesian Association of Municipalities and Districts in its position of 27 November 2015 indicates that new tasks are being imposed on local governments, and that funding sources are required to be provided for the smooth implementation of them: The system of subsidies and financing for local governments should be reformed to ensure diversification of the revenue sources of local government units, including by granting a share of VAT to the JST, which would ensure greater stability of the income of the JST and reduce the impact of business cycles on their revenues.
